135:     ngOnInit(): void {
136:         this.route.params.subscribe(params => {
137:             this.dossierId = params['id'] || '';
138:             if (this.dossierId) {
139:                 localStorage.setItem('lastProjectId', this.dossierId);
140:                 this.bootstrapPage();
141:             }

145:     bootstrapPage(): void {
146:         this.isLoading = true;
147:         forkJoin({
148:             dossier: this.projectsService.getDossier(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null))),
149:             referentiel: forkJoin({
150:                 competences: this.projectsService.getReferentielCompetences(),
151:                 references: this.projectsService.getReferentielReferences(),
152:                 experts: this.projectsService.getReferentielExperts(),
153:                 qualifications: this.projectsService.getReferentielQualifications()
154:             }),
155:             extractionP2: this.projectsService.getExtractionP2(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null))),
156:             matching: this.projectsService.getMatchingResult(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null))),
157:             matrix: this.projectsService.getMatchingMatrix(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null)))
158:         }).subscribe({
159:             next: (data) => {
160:                 this.applyDossierContext(data.dossier);
161:                 this.referentielReady = true;
162:                 this.extractionP2Data = data.extractionP2;
163:                 this.applyMatchingResult(data.matching, data.matrix);
164:                 this.isLoading = false;
165:             },
166:             error: () => {
167:                 this.isLoading = false;
168:             }
169:         });
170:     }
171: 
172:     private applyDossierContext(dossier: any): void {
173:         this.dossierStatus = dossier?.status || '';
174:     }
175: 
176:     private applyMatchingResult(matching: any, matrix: any): void {
177:         const res = matching ?? {};
178:         const mtx = matrix ?? {};
179: 
180:         if (!matching && !matrix) {
181:             this.hasMatchingData = false;
182:             return;
183:         }
184: 
185:         this.hasMatchingData = true;
186: 
187:         this.tauxCompetences = res.tauxCouvertureCompetences ?? mtx.tauxCouvertureCompetences ?? 0;
188:         this.tauxExperts = res.tauxCouvertureExperts ?? mtx.tauxCouvertureExperts ?? 0;
189:         
190:         this.secteurAo = res.secteurAo || mtx.secteurAo || 'Non défini';
191:         this.alignementStrategique = res.alignementStrategique || mtx.alignementStrategique || 'Non évalué';
192:         this.typeContrat = res.typeContrat || '—';
193: 
194:         // Load details
195:         const rawCompetences = this.safeParseJson(res.competencesDetail, []);
196:         if (Array.isArray(rawCompetences) && rawCompetences.length > 0 && rawCompetences[0].score !== undefined) {
197:             this.competencesDetail = rawCompetences;
198:         } else if (Array.isArray(rawCompetences)) {
199:             this.competencesDetail = rawCompetences.map((c: any) => ({
200:                 domaine: c.domaine,
201:                 niveauReferentiel: c.niveauEgis,
202:                 score: c.couvert ? Math.round(70 + Math.random() * 25) : Math.round(20 + Math.random() * 40),
203:                 couvert: c.couvert
204:             }));
205:         }
206: 
207:         this.expertsDetail = this.safeParseJson(res.expertsDetail, []);
208:         this.gapRefs = this.safeParseJson(res.gapRefs || mtx.gapRefs, []);
209: 
210:         const storedMatrix = localStorage.getItem(`matching_matrix_${this.dossierId}`);
211:         if (storedMatrix) {
212:             this.matriceRows = JSON.parse(storedMatrix);
213:         } else {
214:             this.matriceRows = this.safeParseJson(res.matriceDiff || mtx.matriceDiff, []);
215:         }
216: 
217:         const apo = this.extractionP2Data?.apoForm ?? this.extractionP2Data ?? {};
218:         this.partenaires = apo.partenaires || mtx.partenaires || '';
219:         this.chefDeFile = apo.chefDeFile || mtx.chefDeFile || '';
220:     }

230:     runMatching(): void {
231:         this.isRunningMatching = true;
232:         this.matchingProgressStep = 1;
233: 
234:         const progressTimer = setInterval(() => {
235:             if (this.matchingProgressStep < 4) {
236:                 this.matchingProgressStep++;
237:             }
238:         }, 3000);
239: 
240:         this.projectsService.runMatching(this.dossierId).subscribe({
241:             next: () => {
242:                 clearInterval(progressTimer);
243:                 this.matchingProgressStep = 5;
244:                 
245:                 setTimeout(() => {
246:                     this.isRunningMatching = false;
247:                     this.dossierStatus = 'MATCHING';
248:                     localStorage.removeItem(`matching_matrix_${this.dossierId}`);
249: 
250:                     this.messageService.add({ severity: 'success', summary: 'Phase 3 complétée', detail: 'Matrice de différenciation générée.' });
251:                     this.bootstrapPage();
252:                 }, 1000);
253:             },
254:             error: (err) => {
255:                 clearInterval(progressTimer);
256:                 this.isRunningMatching = false;
257:                 this.matchingProgressStep = 0;
258:                 this.messageService.add({ severity: 'error', summary: 'Échec du matching', detail: err?.error?.message || 'Erreur API.' });
259:             }
260:         });
261:     }

265:     // --- Formatters & Helpers ---
266:     
267:     getTauxCompetences(): number { return Math.round(this.tauxCompetences * 100); }
268:     getTauxExperts(): number { return Math.round(this.tauxExperts * 100); }
269:     
270:     calculateRefTaux(): number {
271:         if (!this.gapRefs || this.gapRefs.length === 0) return 0;
272:         let score = 0;
273:         for (const ref of this.gapRefs) {
274:             if (ref.couvert === 'OUI') score += 1;
275:             else if (ref.couvert === 'PARTIEL') score += 0.5;
276:         }
277:         return Math.round((score / this.gapRefs.length) * 100);
278:     }
279:

285:     getStepClass(step: number): string {
286:         if (this.matchingProgressStep === step) return 'step-active';
287:         if (this.matchingProgressStep > step) return 'step-done';
288:         return '';
289:     }
290:     getConnectorClass(step: number): string {
291:         if (this.matchingProgressStep === step) return 'conn-active';
292:         if (this.matchingProgressStep > step) return 'conn-done';

321:     getScoreColor(score: number | undefined): string {

384:     getStatusClass(): string {
385:         const s = this.dossierStatus.toUpperCase();
386:         if (s.includes('MATCHING') || s.includes('DRAFTING')) return 'badge-pastel-green';
387:         return 'badge-pastel-blue';
388:     }
389:     getStatusLabel(): string {
390:         return this.dossierStatus === 'MATCHING' ? 'Terminé' : 'En attente';
391:     }
392: 
393:     // --- Matrice Edition ---
394:     getMatrixCoverage(): number {
395:         if (!this.matriceRows || this.matriceRows.length === 0) return 0;
396:         let covered = 0;
397:         for (const row of this.matriceRows) {
398:             if (row.positionEgis === 'COUVERT') covered += 1;
399:             else if (row.positionEgis === 'PARTIELLEMENT' || row.positionEgis === 'VIA_PARTENAIRE') covered += 0.5;
400:         }
401:         return Math.round((covered / this.matriceRows.length) * 100);
402:     }
403:     getCoveredCount(): number {
404:         return this.matriceRows.filter(r => r.positionEgis === 'COUVERT' || r.positionEgis === 'VIA_PARTENAIRE').length;
405:     }
406: 
407:     editRow(index: number): void { this.editingRows[index] = true; }
408:     saveRow(index: number): void {
409:         this.editingRows[index] = false;
410:         localStorage.setItem(`matching_matrix_${this.dossierId}`, JSON.stringify(this.matriceRows));
411:         this.projectsService.updateMatchingMatrix(this.dossierId, this.matriceRows).subscribe();
412:     }
413:     addRow(): void {
414:         this.matriceRows.push({ critere: 'Nouveau critère', positionEgis: 'COUVERT', argumentGap: '' });
415:         this.editRow(this.matriceRows.length - 1);
416:     }
417:     onPositionChange(index: number, value: string): void {}
418: 
419:     // --- Champs Strat ---
420:     updateField(fieldName: string, value: string): void {
421:         if (!this.dossierId || !value) return;
422:         
423:         // Mettre à jour localement pour l'aperçu APO
424:         if (this.extractionP2Data) {
425:             if (this.extractionP2Data.apoForm) {
426:                 this.extractionP2Data.apoForm[fieldName] = value;
427:             } else {
428:                 this.extractionP2Data[fieldName] = value;
429:             }
430:         }
431:         
432:         this.projectsService.updateApoField(this.dossierId, fieldName, value).subscribe();
433:     }
434:     updateRelationClient(value: string): void {
435:         const levelIndex = this.relationClientList.indexOf(value);
436:         let level = 1;
437:         if (levelIndex === 2) level = 5; 
438:         else if (levelIndex === 1) level = 3;
439:         this.updateField('RELATION_CLIENT', level.toString());
440:     }
441: 
442:     // --- Décision ---
443:     isAlignementOk(): boolean {
444:         return this.alignementStrategique !== 'Non aligné';
445:     }
446:     isCompatible(): boolean {
447:         return this.getTauxCompetences() >= 50 && this.calculateRefTaux() >= 50 && this.getTauxExperts() >= 40 && this.isAlignementOk();
448:     }

450:     generateMethodology(): void {
451:         this.projectsService.assembleApo(this.dossierId).subscribe({
452:             next: () => {

454:                 this.projectsService.updateStatus(this.dossierId, 'DRAFTING').subscribe({
455:                     next: () => this.router.navigate(['/dossiers', this.dossierId, 'rapport-final']),
456:                     error: () => this.router.navigate(['/dossiers', this.dossierId, 'rapport-final'])
457:                 });
458:             }
459:         });
460:     }
461: 
462:     confirmNoGo(): void {
463:         this.projectsService.confirmNoGo(this.dossierId).subscribe({
464:             next: () => {
465:                 this.projectsService.generateNoGoReport(this.dossierId).subscribe({
466:                     next: () => {
467:                         this.messageService.add({ severity: 'success', summary: 'No-Go Confirmé', detail: 'Rapport généré.' });
468:                         this.router.navigate(['/dossiers', this.dossierId, 'no-go-report']);
469:                     }
470:                 });
471:             }
472:         });
473:     }
474: 
475:     forceCompatible(): void {
476:         this.projectsService.forceCompatible(this.dossierId, 'Décision forcée par l\'analyste').subscribe({
477:             next: () => {
478:                 this.messageService.add({ severity: 'success', summary: 'Forçage réussi', detail: 'Le dossier passe en drafting méthodologie.' });
479:                 this.generateMethodology();
480:             }
481:         });
482:     }

484:     private safeParseJson(data: any, fallback: any): any {
485:         if (!data) return fallback;
486:         if (typeof data === 'object') return data;
487:         try { return JSON.parse(data); } catch { return fallback; }
488:     }
489: 
490:     getApoPreviewData(): Record<string, string> {
491:         return this.extractionP2Data?.apoForm ?? this.extractionP2Data ?? {};
492:     }
493: 
494:     showDocxPreview(): void {
495:         this.showApoPreviewDialog = true;
496:         this.isMethodologyGenerated = false;
497:         this.isGeneratingMethodology = false;
498:         this.isDocxLoading = true;
499:         this.docxLoadError = false;
500:         this.errorMessageForUI = '';
501: 
502:         // On va chercher "Methodologie-Template1.docx" car c'est votre VRAI fichier non vide !
503:         // (L'autre étant verrouillé par le serveur backend).
504:         const templateToFetch = 'Methodologie-Template1.docx';
505: 
506:         this.projectsService.getTemplateDocx(templateToFetch).subscribe({
507:             next: (blob) => {
508:                 if (blob.type.includes('html') || blob.size < 100) {
509:                     console.error('Blob is too small, likely an error page.');
510:                     this.errorMessageForUI = 'Le backend a renvoyé un fichier invalide ou vide (' + blob.size + ' octets). Type: ' + blob.type;
511:                     this.docxLoadError = true;
512:                     this.isDocxLoading = false;
513:                     return;
514:                 }
515:                 
516:                 setTimeout(() => {
517:                     if (this.docxContainer && this.docxContainer.nativeElement) {
518:                         try {
519:                             this.docxContainer.nativeElement.innerHTML = ''; // clear previous
520:                             docx.renderAsync(blob, this.docxContainer.nativeElement, null, {
521:                                 className: 'docx',
522:                                 inWrapper: true,
523:                                 ignoreWidth: false,
524:                                 ignoreHeight: false,
525:                                 ignoreFonts: false,
526:                                 breakPages: true,
527:                                 ignoreLastRenderedPageBreak: true,
528:                                 experimental: true,
529:                                 trimXmlDeclaration: true,
530:                                 debug: false,
531:                             }).then(() => {
532:                                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
533:                             }).catch(err => {
534:                                 console.error('Error rendering docx', err);
535:                                 this.errorMessageForUI = 'Erreur lors de la lecture du DOCX (fichier corrompu ou format non supporté).';
536:                                 this.docxLoadError = true;
537:                                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
538:                             });
539:                         } catch (e: any) {
540:                             console.error('Exception rendering docx', e);
541:                             this.errorMessageForUI = 'Exception Javascript : ' + (e.message || 'Erreur inconnue');
542:                             this.docxLoadError = true;
543:                             this.isDocxLoading = false;
544:                         }
545:                     } else {
546:                         this.errorMessageForUI = 'Le conteneur HTML du document est introuvable.';
547:                         this.docxLoadError = true;
548:                         this.isDocxLoading = false;
549:                     }
550:                 }, 400);
551:             },
552:             error: (err) => {
553:                 console.error('API Error fetching DOCX', err);
554:                 this.errorMessageForUI = 'Erreur réseau/API : ' + (err.message || 'Impossible de contacter le serveur');
555:                 this.docxLoadError = true;
556:                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
557:             }
558:         });
559:     }
560: 
561: 
562: 
563:     downloadDocxTemplate(): void {
564:         this.projectsService.getTemplateDocx('Methodologie-Template1.docx').subscribe(blob => {
565:             this.projectsService.triggerBrowserDownload(blob, 'Methodologie-Template1.docx');
566:         });
567:     }

147:     bootstrapPage(): void {
148:         this.isLoading = true;
149:         forkJoin({
150:             dossier: this.projectsService.getDossier(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null))),
151:             referentiel: forkJoin({
152:                 competences: this.projectsService.getReferentielCompetences(),
153:                 references: this.projectsService.getReferentielReferences(),
154:                 experts: this.projectsService.getReferentielExperts(),
155:                 qualifications: this.projectsService.getReferentielQualifications()
156:             }),
157:             extractionP2: this.projectsService.getExtractionP2(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null))),
158:             matching: this.projectsService.getMatchingResult(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null))),
159:             matrix: this.projectsService.getMatchingMatrix(this.dossierId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null)))
160:         }).subscribe({
161:             next: (data) => {
162:                 this.applyDossierContext(data.dossier);
163:                 this.referentielReady = true;
164:                 this.extractionP2Data = data.extractionP2;
165:                 this.applyMatchingResult(data.matching, data.matrix);
166:                 this.isLoading = false;
167:             },
168:             error: () => {
169:                 this.isLoading = false;
170:             }
171:         });
172:     }

178:     private applyMatchingResult(matching: any, matrix: any): void {
179:         const res = matching ?? {};
180:         const mtx = matrix ?? {};
181: 
182:         if (!matching && !matrix) {
183:             this.hasMatchingData = false;
184:             return;
185:         }
186: 
187:         this.hasMatchingData = true;
188: 
189:         this.tauxCompetences = res.tauxCouvertureCompetences ?? mtx.tauxCouvertureCompetences ?? 0;
190:         this.tauxExperts = res.tauxCouvertureExperts ?? mtx.tauxCouvertureExperts ?? 0;
191:         
192:         this.secteurAo = res.secteurAo || mtx.secteurAo || 'Non dÃ©fini';
193:         this.alignementStrategique = res.alignementStrategique || mtx.alignementStrategique || 'Non Ã©valuÃ©';
194:         this.typeContrat = res.typeContrat || 'â€”';
195: 
196:         // Load details
197:         const rawCompetences = this.safeParseJson(res.competencesDetail, []);
198:         if (Array.isArray(rawCompetences) && rawCompetences.length > 0 && rawCompetences[0].score !== undefined) {
199:             this.competencesDetail = rawCompetences;
200:         } else if (Array.isArray(rawCompetences)) {
201:             this.competencesDetail = rawCompetences.map((c: any) => ({
202:                 domaine: c.domaine,
203:                 niveauReferentiel: c.niveauEgis,
204:                 score: c.couvert ? Math.round(70 + Math.random() * 25) : Math.round(20 + Math.random() * 40),
205:                 couvert: c.couvert
206:             }));
207:         }
208: 
209:         this.expertsDetail = this.safeParseJson(res.expertsDetail, []);
210:         this.gapRefs = this.safeParseJson(res.gapRefs || mtx.gapRefs, []);
211: 
212:         const storedMatrix = sessionStorage.getItem(`matching_matrix_${this.dossierId}`);
213:         if (storedMatrix) {
214:             this.matriceRows = JSON.parse(storedMatrix);
215:         } else {
216:             this.matriceRows = this.safeParseJson(res.matriceDiff || mtx.matriceDiff, []);
217:         }
218: 
219:         const apo = this.extractionP2Data?.apoForm ?? this.extractionP2Data ?? {};
220:         this.partenaires = apo.partenaires || mtx.partenaires || '';
221:         this.chefDeFile = apo.chefDeFile || mtx.chefDeFile || '';
222:     }

232:     runMatching(): void {

236:         const progressTimer = setInterval(() => {
237:             if (this.matchingProgressStep < 4) {
238:                 this.matchingProgressStep++;

240:         }, 3000);
241: 
242:         this.projectsService.runMatching(this.dossierId).subscribe({
243:             next: () => {

245:                 this.matchingProgressStep = 5;
246:                 
247:                 setTimeout(() => {
248:                     this.isRunningMatching = false;
249:                     this.dossierStatus = 'MATCHING';
250:                     sessionStorage.removeItem(`matching_matrix_${this.dossierId}`);
251: 
252:                     this.messageService.add({ severity: 'success', summary: 'Phase 3 complÃ©tÃ©e', detail: 'Matrice de diffÃ©renciation gÃ©nÃ©rÃ©e.' });
253:                     this.bootstrapPage();
254:                 }, 1000);
255:             },
256:             error: (err) => {
257:                 clearInterval(progressTimer);
258:                 this.isRunningMatching = false;
259:                 this.matchingProgressStep = 0;
260:                 this.messageService.add({ severity: 'error', summary: 'Ã‰chec du matching', detail: err?.error?.message || 'Erreur API.' });
261:             }
262:         });

272:     calculateRefTaux(): number {

277:             else if (ref.couvert === 'PARTIEL') score += 0.5;
278:         }
279:         return Math.round((score / this.gapRefs.length) * 100);
280:     }

396:     getMatrixCoverage(): number {

398:         let covered = 0;

491:     private safeParseJson(data: any, fallback: any): any {
492:         if (!data) return fallback;
493:         if (typeof data === 'object') return data;
494:         try { return JSON.parse(data); } catch { return fallback; }
495:     }

501:     showDocxPreview(): void {
502:         this.showApoPreviewDialog = true;
503:         this.isMethodologyGenerated = false;
504:         this.isGeneratingMethodology = false;
505:         this.isDocxLoading = true;
506:         this.docxLoadError = false;
507:         this.errorMessageForUI = '';
508: 
509:         // On va chercher "Methodologie-Template1.docx" car c'est votre VRAI fichier non vide !
510:         // (L'autre Ã©tant verrouillÃ© par le serveur backend).
511:         const templateToFetch = 'Methodologie-Template1.docx';
512: 
513:         this.projectsService.getTemplateDocx(templateToFetch).subscribe({
514:             next: (blob) => {
515:                 if (blob.type.includes('html') || blob.size < 100) {
516:                     console.error('Blob is too small, likely an error page.');
517:                     this.errorMessageForUI = 'Le backend a renvoyÃ© un fichier invalide ou vide (' + blob.size + ' octets). Type: ' + blob.type;
518:                     this.docxLoadError = true;
519:                     this.isDocxLoading = false;
520:                     return;
521:                 }
522:                 
523:                 setTimeout(() => {
524:                     if (this.docxContainer && this.docxContainer.nativeElement) {
525:                         try {
526:                             this.docxContainer.nativeElement.innerHTML = ''; // clear previous
527:                             docx.renderAsync(blob, this.docxContainer.nativeElement, null, {
528:                                 className: 'docx',
529:                                 inWrapper: true,
530:                                 ignoreWidth: false,
531:                                 ignoreHeight: false,
532:                                 ignoreFonts: false,
533:                                 breakPages: true,
534:                                 ignoreLastRenderedPageBreak: true,
535:                                 experimental: true,
536:                                 trimXmlDeclaration: true,
537:                                 debug: false,
538:                             }).then(() => {
539:                                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
540:                             }).catch(err => {
541:                                 console.error('Error rendering docx', err);
542:                                 this.errorMessageForUI = 'Erreur lors de la lecture du DOCX (fichier corrompu ou format non supportÃ©).';
543:                                 this.docxLoadError = true;
544:                                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
545:                             });
546:                         } catch (e: any) {
547:                             console.error('Exception rendering docx', e);
548:                             this.errorMessageForUI = 'Exception Javascript : ' + (e.message || 'Erreur inconnue');
549:                             this.docxLoadError = true;
550:                             this.isDocxLoading = false;
551:                         }
552:                     } else {
553:                         this.errorMessageForUI = 'Le conteneur HTML du document est introuvable.';
554:                         this.docxLoadError = true;
555:                         this.isDocxLoading = false;
556:                     }
557:                 }, 400);
558:             },
559:             error: (err) => {
560:                 console.error('API Error fetching DOCX', err);
561:                 this.errorMessageForUI = 'Erreur rÃ©seau/API : ' + (err.message || 'Impossible de contacter le serveur');
562:                 this.docxLoadError = true;
563:                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
564:             }
565:         });
566:     }

178:     private applyMatchingResult(matching: any, matrix: any): void {

201:         const rawCompetences = this.safeParseJson(res.competencesDetail, []);
202:         if (Array.isArray(rawCompetences) && rawCompetences.length > 0 && rawCompetences[0].score !== undefined) {
203:             this.competencesDetail = rawCompetences;

209:                 couvert: c.couvert
210:             }));
211:         }

531:                             docx.renderAsync(blob, this.docxContainer.nativeElement, null, {
532:                                 className: 'docx',
533:                                 inWrapper: true,
534:                                 ignoreWidth: false,
535:                                 ignoreHeight: false,
536:                                 ignoreFonts: false,

541:                                 debug: false,
542:                             }).then(() => {
543:                                 this.isDocxLoading = false;
544:                             }).catch(err => {
545:                                 console.error('Error rendering docx', err);
546:                                 this.errorMessageForUI = 'Erreur lors de la lecture du DOCX (fichier corrompu ou format non supportÃ©).';

553:                             this.docxLoadError = true;
554:                             this.isDocxLoading = false;
555:                         }
556:                     } else {
557:                         this.errorMessageForUI = 'Le conteneur HTML du document est introuvable.';
558:                         this.docxLoadError = true;
559:                         this.isDocxLoading = false;
560:                     }
561:                 }, 400);
562:             },
563:             error: (err) => {
564:                 console.error('API Error fetching DOCX', err);
565:                 this.errorMessageForUI = 'Erreur rÃ©seau/API : ' + (err.message || 'Impossible de contacter le serveur');
566:                 this.docxLoadError = true;

423:         for (const row of this.matriceRows) {
424:             if (row.positionEgis === 'COUVERT') covered += 1;
425:             else if (row.positionEgis === 'PARTIELLEMENT' || row.positionEgis === 'VIA_PARTENAIRE') covered += 0.5;
426:         }
427:         return Math.round((covered / this.matriceRows.length) * 100);
428:     }
429:     getCoveredCount(): number {
430:         return this.matriceRows.filter(r => r.positionEgis === 'COUVERT' || r.positionEgis === 'VIA_PARTENAIRE').length;
431:     }
432: 
433:     editRow(index: number): void { this.editingRows[index] = true; }
434:     saveRow(index: number): void {
435:         this.editingRows[index] = false;
436:         sessionStorage.setItem(`matching_matrix_${this.dossierId}`, JSON.stringify(this.matriceRows));
437:         this.projectsService.updateMatchingMatrix(this.dossierId, this.matriceRows).subscribe();
438:     }
439:     addRow(): void {

441:         this.editRow(this.matriceRows.length - 1);
442:     }
443:     onPositionChange(index: number, value: string): void {}
444: 
445:     // --- Champs Strat ---
446:     updateField(fieldName: string, value: string): void {

448:

452:                 this.extractionP2Data.apoForm[fieldName] = value;
453:             } else {
454:                 this.extractionP2Data[fieldName] = value;
455:             }
456:         }
457:         
458:         this.projectsService.updateApoField(this.dossierId, fieldName, value).subscribe();
459:     }
460:     updateRelationClient(value: string): void {
461:         const levelIndex = this.relationClientList.indexOf(value);
462:         let level = 1;
463:         if (levelIndex === 2) level = 5; 
464:         else if (levelIndex === 1) level = 3;
465:         this.updateField('RELATION_CLIENT', level.toString());
466:     }
467: 
468:     // --- DÃ©cision ---
469:     isAlignementOk(): boolean {
470:         return this.alignementStrategique !== 'Non alignÃ©';
471:     }
472:     isCompatible(): boolean {
473:         return this.getTauxCompetences() >= 50 && this.calculateRefTaux() >= 50 && this.getTauxExperts() >= 40 && this.isAlignementOk();
474:     }
475: 
476:     generateMethodology(): void {
477:         this.projectsService.assembleApo(this.dossierId).subscribe({
478:             next: () => {
479:                 this.messageService.add({ severity: 'success', summary: 'GÃ©nÃ©ration en cours', detail: 'La mÃ©thodologie est gÃ©nÃ©rÃ©e.' });

483:                 });
484:             }
485:         });
486:     }

502:             }
503:         });
504:     }

510:                 this.generateMethodology();
511:             }
